| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/JournalBackupAdapter.swift` | `JournalBackupAdapter`, `JournalBackupSeamClosedError`, `JournalBackupDayStoreUnreadableError`, `SealedBackupJournalReferences` and `SealedDeviceKeyRead` — the journal payload's v2 adapter (design 2026-09-30, §7, unit B3) over the sealed `JournalNarrativeRepository`: the snapshot is the sealed ids some day's journals still reference plus every sealed entry on a day whose stored row will not decode (`SealedBackupContext.sealedBackupJournalReferences` → `SealedBackupJournalReferences`, the keyless `JournalNarrativeRepository.ids(onDays:)`; an orphan row on a readable day is never exported, and an undecodable day row never stops the export — B3 fix round 2; nil while the day store's read cannot account for every row, and the snapshot then throws `JournalBackupDayStoreUnreadableError` — the export fails with nothing written, B3 fix round 1), each chunk read under the hub key OR the journal device key read without minting (`SealedDeviceKeyRead`, the helper the "can't open" check shares), the id-keyed fork merge, the day skeletons rebuilt from the keyless `skeletons(ids:)` (false keeps the restore unresolved), "Remove them" re-classifying under every key before a keyless delete; its seam is `!duress` (journaling has no hide switch). A v1 chunk is a bare `[JournalNarrative]`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedBackupBookkeeping.swift` | `SealedBackupBookkeeping` — the per-payload, per-install v2 bookkeeping (§4.3): the restore marker (`fernlet.cycleRecord.periodRestoreResolved`, seeded once from the legacy latch, never resolved because sync or the backup is off), the install-bound accepted head (`fernlet.sealedBackup.periodAcceptedHead`, `"<acceptor>:<writer>:<generation>:<salt8>"`, kept by delete-all) the observed foreign head (`fernlet.sealedBackup.periodObservedHead`, `"<acceptor>:<writer>:<generation>"`) and the in-flight generation (`fernlet.sealedBackup.periodInFlight`, `"<acceptor>:<generation>"`: the highest generation this install's commits set out to save, recorded before the first save, only ever raised, cleared only by an uninstall — E2's bound on "own"); one literal key per switch arm. `SealedBackupAcceptedHead` (the unchanged-metadata check — generation AND a non-empty salt prefix). **Unit B2:** the intimate-log arms — `fernlet.intimacyLog.restoreResolved` (seeded once from `fernlet.intimacyLog.everStored`), `fernlet.sealedBackup.intimacyAcceptedHead`, `…intimacyObservedHead`, `…intimacyInFlight` — and `v2Payloads` = period, intimate logs. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedBackupV2Format.swift` | The v2 plaintext every v2 payload shares (§5.1): `SealedBackupV2Envelope` (the frozen `v` / `writer` / `set` / `total` / `records` envelope, writer and set in EVERY chunk), `SealedBackupV2EnvelopeHeader`, `SealedBackupV2Format` (v1 detection, header, encode), `SealedBackupV2FormatError`, `SealedBackupHeadStamp` (writer + generation; `"v1"` for a bare array), `SealedBackupWriterTag` (16 bytes of `SHA256(Hash.sealedBackupWriterTagV1 ‖ DeviceBindingID)`), `SealedBackupSetTag` (16 random bytes per pass) and `PeriodBackupExportState` (what Privacy & Data names for the period backup; `derive` is the one status-to-state mapping the store and the tests share — a restore waiting for its key from iCloud Keychain is `waitingForKey`, named as waiting and never as a set sealed with another key; one whose set will not authenticate is `sealedWithAnotherKey`; both offer "Start a new backup"; one refused as older names that set with "Restore it here" and "Replace"). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedPhotoBackupService.swift` | `SealedPhotoCrypto` (AAD **v3**, per-photo) + `SealedPhotoBackupService` — the own-photo escrow route's mechanism half (hardening Phase 5, step 5b): seal one record per photo id, write the per-corpus manifest LAST as the commit marker, restore manifest-first (authenticate → generation high-water → fetch bodies), incremental add, delete, corpus teardown. Derives on the same v2 salted escrow key as the chunked route; a **distinct namespace**, never a `SealedBackupPayloadType` case. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OwnPhotoBackupCoordinator.swift` | `OwnPhotoBackupCoordinator` (`OwnPhotoBackupContext`) — the own-photo escrow route's policy half: the single opt-in preference, the per-corpus FILE-PRESENCE no-clobber gate, restore-before-reupload ordering, the progress corpus's sealed-index sidecar, the delete-all teardown, and `OwnPhotoUploadLedger` (the device-local record of which ids THIS device uploaded — the prune scope that keeps one device's pass from deleting another device's photos). Owns its own `MealPhotoStore`/`ProgressPhotoStore` instances over `OwnPhotoCorpusLayout`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DuressRecoveryCoordinator.swift` | `DuressRecoveryCoordinator` (+ `DuressRecoveryRequest` / `DuressRecoveryReply` / `DuressRecoveryTranscript` / `DuressRecoveryDecision` / `DuressRecoveryOutcome` / `DuressRecoveryError`) — the app-side half of `DuressMode.recoveryLock` (security-hardening Phase 7, step 8): the in-person QR mutual-auth ceremonies that enroll the user's own second device as a recovery custodian and later get the content key back from it. Lives in the app target because it needs BOTH `FernletLock` and `ProximityKit` and the lock must gain no ProximityKit edge — the content key is released into `IdentityService.seal` through a closure, never returned. One type plays both roles (custodian displays, primary scans). Transport-free: every step returns the bytes to send. **No cloud route of any kind** — recovery is in-person mesh + QR only. Since ProximityKit plan step A0.2.5 it scans and builds, signs and checks the verify response under its identity's namespace (`identity.namespace`, `identity.purposes`), which makes `fernlet.verify.response.v1` ProximityKit's label, supplied by Fernlet's namespace; the two duress labels stay the app's. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DuressPINSetupView.swift` | `DuressPINSetupView` (+ `DuressSetupAvailability` / `DuressModeCopy`) — Settings → App lock → **Duress code** (security-hardening Phase 7, step 9): choose one `DuressMode`, set/change the code, enrol or remove the recovery device, remove the code. Real-PIN-gated **by construction** (the page it is pushed from is `.appLockSettings`-gated), so it takes no `current:` passcode. `DuressSetupAvailability` is the view-free gating policy — `.recoveryLock` unselectable without an enrolled custodian, the recovery device unremovable while `.recoveryLock` is armed — split out so those two rules are unit-testable. `DuressModeCopy` is the destructive-path copy, reviewed as a unit; service rejections are surfaced verbatim, never restated. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DuressRecoveryCeremonyViews.swift` | `DuressRecoveryEnrollmentSheet` / `DuressRecoveryReturnSheet` (+ `DuressCeremonyMessage` / `DuressCeremonyQR`) — the in-person screens that drive `DuressRecoveryCoordinator`, for both roles on both phones. Transport is a **QR relay**, not the mesh: the coordinator is transport-free, so the bytes ride codes on screen and ProximityKit gains no new `PayloadType`. The relay adds no trust — the small hops carry only public nonces and public keys (a substituted key produces a response the real scanner rejects), and the large hops are already sealed and signed by the coordinator. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DeleteAllDataConfirmation.swift` | `DeleteAllDataConfirmation` — the single shared destructive confirm dialog for "delete everything", funneling both Settings entry points to `FernletStore.deleteAllData`. Its scope sentence names "photos from a Friends session you haven't chosen yet" (leg 4d, 2026-09-30) under a new key, `deleteAll.scope.withUnchosenSessionPhotos`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DeleteEverythingFlow.swift` | `DeleteEverythingFlow` — per-screen "delete everything" wipe state (busy/success/failure) + confirmation glue and the shared `.deleteEverythingAlerts` outcome alerts, instantiated once per entry point (`SettingsSheet`, `PrivacyDataSettingsView`); buttons/overlays/dismiss guards stay in the views. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PrivacyPolicyView.swift` | `PrivacyPolicyView` — in-app Privacy Policy screen: a static policy string (not catalog-backed) parsed by `PolicyMarkdown` and rendered in the app's voice/type; kept in step with `Docs/Privacy-Policy.md` and `Site/privacy/index.html` by `PrivacyPolicyParityTests` (same effective date, same load-bearing clauses). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PrivacyDataSettingsView.swift` | Privacy and data settings screen for managing storage preferences, HealthKit toggles, and sealed backup options. Routes every destructive/OFF toggle through a pre-commit warning; surfaces the sealed-backup restore status + escrow-conflict banner (retry / re-link). Also carries the own-photo escrow switch (with its size disclosure) and the step-5c "Lock photos to this device" consent ceremony — the branch that lets a user device-bind their own photos without turning the iCloud photo backup on. Holds the "Forget corrected searches" row (research §26 fix 1.10's escape hatch — clears the local correction memory and nothing else, behind the same `DestructiveConfirmation`, hidden when nothing is remembered). After an app-lock reset it says the Sealed backup is kept as it was and offers the owner's "Restore" (it releases the hold), in place of upload promises the hold makes untrue. Entered through the same fresh device-owner check with or without a Fernlet passcode (period-data design Q5; a missing iPhone passcode admits only a no-passcode install), with the delete card beneath the check. Names the period backup's states (§10.6): another iPhone's set — or a v1 set, which names no iPhone — ("Restore it here" of exactly that set behind its confirmation, "Replace it with this iPhone's history" behind a destructive one), a restore waiting for its key from iCloud Keychain and a set sealed with a backup key this iPhone doesn't have (each with "Start a new backup" behind its own confirmation, which says to wait for the key unless iCloud Keychain was reset or the backup is damaged; the "other iPhone keeps its own entries" Replace confirmation only for a named set), entries that cannot open, and "will catch up the next time you open the Cycle page". Releases, on entry, a reset hold that keeps no enabled backup's copy (no false "Restore" prompt). **Unit B2:** the intimate-log backup speaks through `SealedBackupV2StatusRows` with `.intimacy` words (`FernletStore.intimacyBackupRowState`; nothing while hidden, under 16 or in duress): "Restore it here" / "Restore anyway" behind a non-destructive confirmation, Replace, Start a new backup and Remove them behind destructive ones, "Open Private to finish." announced once; its turn-off confirmation keeps a slot observed as another iPhone's ("Stop backing up this iPhone's intimate logs?") and otherwise says the backup is deleted for every iPhone that uses it; the Retry predicate no longer ORs in the intimacy flag (R2-F9), and the intimacy pre-reset copy is never "blocked" (a merge). **Unit B3:** the journal backup speaks through the same rows with `.journal` words (`FernletStore.journalBackupRowState`; nothing during a duress session), its restore confirmation naming the fork rule ("If an entry was changed on both iPhones, you'll see both versions") and that deleted entries may come back; the journal turn-off confirmations mirror the intimate-log ones; the Retry predicate no longer ORs in the journal flag, the old "hasn't finished uploading" line and the pre-reset "can't be restored" lines are gone (every restore is a merge); one generic confirm/run path (`confirmV2BackupChoice`, `v2DestructiveConfirmation`, `runV2BackupChoice`, `v2RestoreAlert`) serves both kinds. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedBackupV2RowState.swift` | `SealedBackupV2RowState` — the ONE mapping from a v2 payload's engine status (and, after a relaunch, the persisted owner hold, restore marker, observed head and owed upload, in that order) to a Privacy & Data row (design 2026-09-30, §10.1): none, finishing ("Open Private to finish."), waiting for the restore, waiting for the key, another iPhone's set, older than seen, sealed with another key, damaged, needs a newer Fernlet (also for a restore that found a set only a newer Fernlet reads — never the "next time" promise, B3 fix round 1), paused, too large, failed, catch-up. Used by the intimate-log rows (unit B2); the journal joins in B3. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedBackupV2StatusRows.swift` | `SealedBackupV2StatusRows`, `SealedBackupV2RowCopy` and `SealedBackupV2RowChoice` — the intimate-log (unit B2) and journal (unit B3) Sealed backup rows inside Privacy & Data's banner (design 2026-09-30, §10.1): one whole-sentence catalog entry per `SealedBackupV2RowState`, written per kind in its `SealedBackupV2RowCopy` (`.intimacy`, `.journal`; never a spliced noun) with its frozen identifiers (`privacy.sealedBackup.<kind>.<state>`, the catch-up line keeping `…intimacyDeferred` / `…journalDeferred`), one accessibility element per row, 44 pt buttons with hints ("Restore it here", "Replace it with this iPhone's …", "Restore anyway", "Start a new backup", "Remove them"), the choice handed to the parent to confirm. Shown only while the kind is visible and no duress session runs. (Was `IntimacyBackupStatusRows.swift`.) |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DestructiveConfirmation.swift` | Reusable destructive-action confirmation (`DestructiveConfirmation` + `.destructiveConfirmation` modifier): the mutation runs only on an explicit destructive-role confirm, so no destructive settings toggle can ship without a warning. |

## Data, Persistence, And State

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/` | Core domain models (formerly the app's `Models.swift`), split per area — see the Domain Models section. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore.swift` | Main observable app store that coordinates repository data and app-level state changes. Holds the ordered `deleteAllData` wipe funnel, whose **leg 0** (P6 item 4's fix review) calls `MeshNetworkManager.beginPrivacyWipe()` — the manager's one transcript-clear funnel, which also bumps `transcriptGeneration` and switches the routed projection off for the rest of the wipe — paired with `endPrivacyWipe()` in a `defer`. **P7 item 2:** owns `applyProximityRunPolicy(scenePhase:protectedDataAvailable:duressSessionActive:now:)` (the `appLockEngaged:` label retired by P9-3-A, 2026-09-22) — THE run-policy funnel and the only writer of `MeshNetworkManager.applyRoutedAccessGate(_:now:)` outside ProximityKit (the lock facts are parameters, read at the edge, never the lagging mirrors) — plus the `selectedTab` mirror, the memory-only `deleteAllInProgress` bracket flag raised inside the wipe funnel, and the kept `proximityRunVerdict`. **P7 item 3** split the funnel into a private core (`runProximityPolicy(_:now:)` — input, verdict, the gate write, then the seams' executor; since session photos U3 fix round 1 (U3-L-U3-R1) the input is sampled TWICE, the gate written from the first and the radio half — and the kept verdict — decided over a second sample taken after the gate's pass, because that pass can make an ended session's photo review outstanding and so raise `sessionPhotoReviewBlocksDiscovery`) behind three entries: the scene edge (as before, retaining its three facts), the view edge (`applyProximityRunPolicy(duressSessionActive:now:)`, a fresh duress fact over the retained scene facts) and the store edge (`reapplyProximityRunPolicy(now:)`, everything retained; before any scene edge both assume the most restrictive scene). The two nearby opt-in setters and the wipe funnel's raise and lower of `deleteAllInProgress` run the policy instead of calling a manager; `discoveryTimeoutTask` (the tab's five-minute timeout) lives here for the seams. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/StoreCore/AIRetryQueueService.swift` | AI analysis retry queue; stores pending retry records and calls `onChange` on each mutation. Extracted sub-service of `FernletStore`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/StoreCore/DerivedSignalsService.swift` | Derived signal computation and deferred post-launch rebuild scheduling. Extracted sub-service of `FernletStore`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/StoreCore/DerivedSignalsRebuilder.swift` | Pure helper that rebuilds derived signals from a historical day window; used by `DerivedSignalsService`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/StoreCore/SavedRecipeService.swift` | Observable saved recipe list manager; loads, adds, deletes, and persists saved recipes. Extracted sub-service of `FernletStore`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/StoreCore/SnapshotSaveCoordinator.swift` | Debounced snapshot persistence coordinator and remote-change reload handler. Extracted sub-service of `FernletStore`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/StoreCore/PendingWriteBuffer.swift` | `DebouncedRowBuffer` / `DebouncedAppendBuffer` — the queue-then-debounce-then-flush write machinery, and its durability contract (the pending queue is the sole un-persisted copy, so it is cleared only after a confirmed write and a failed write keeps it for retry instead of trapping), shared by `SavedRecipeService` / `CustomItemService` (upsert+delete rows) and `CoinLedgerService` / `MilestoneLedgerService` (append-only rows). The one home of what those four services previously carried as byte-identical copies; row meaning, load-time dedup, and reset semantics stay in each service. Plus `PendingResetBoundaries` (2026-09-24): the durable reset-boundary half both ledgers share — remember before the delete, land and retire, merge what is still pending on every load. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStoreLoader.swift` | Async store bootstrap coordinator that manages `preparing → ready → failed` phase transitions at launch; its once-per-process `startIfNeeded()` first runs `ProximityLiveActivityReaper.endOrphans()` (before any proximity manager can exist), then loads the store. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStoreAccess.swift` | `FernletStoreAccess` — the one process-global store-acquisition cache, shared by the scene loader (via `ExchangeIntentService.loadStoreForUI`) and the background App Intents. Main-actor isolated and single-in-flight: a cold background launch and the scene loader coalesce onto one `load()` rather than building competing `FernletStore`s over the same repositories. Lifted out of `ExchangeIntentService.swift` by the P10 mesh round so the companion background-refresh handler can share it. Its missing `deinit` is allowlisted by `MemoryLifecycleBoundaryTests` (ML1) with the invariant that makes it safe. **P10 item 4** adds the LATE ATTACH (decision D-10.4.1): the cache returns its store whatever arguments a later `load()` passes, and the scene loader passes the app's one long-lived `HealthKitService` while every background caller passes nil — so a cold background wake that built the store first left the foreground on a store with no gateway for the rest of the process, its workout sync silently falling back to a second instance of its own. `load()` now attaches a caller's gateway to a cached or coalesced store that has none, through `FernletStore.attachHealthKitServiceIfMissing(_:)`, and audits when it does. The repair lives here rather than in the refresh handler because the handler may not speak a HealthKit spelling at all (§16.4's wall), and because the background App Intents have had the same defect since they were written — the refresh only moved it from a rare Shortcut to a fifteen-minute cadence. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DataExportBuilder.swift` | `FernletDataExport` + `FernletStore` builder — assembles the user's non-sealed data into one human-readable JSON export file (allowlist projection; sealed/sensitive data excluded by construction). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/TrainerExportBuilder.swift` | `TrainerExportBundle` / `TrainerExportOptions` / `TrainerExportWindow` + `FernletStore` builder — curated fail-closed-allowlist workout + nutrition export for a trainer/nutritionist, with opt-in extras. Also carries macro targets, training setup (location/equipment/split), and the per-exercise progression rollup (`ExerciseLineParser`). The window defaults to unlimited (file/mesh) and narrows for the clipboard. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+DemoSeed.swift` | DEBUG-only `FernletStore` extension that seeds today's diary with representative demo content (day-idempotent) so every tab renders populated cards for the UX appearance UI tests. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/LocalFernletRepository.swift` | Local JSON-style repository contract and implementation, snapshot/database records, derived signal creation, limits, and memory engine logic. `loadAllDaysWithUnreadable()` does not account for every row in read-only recovery (B3 fix rounds 1 and 2). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/CoreDataFernletRepository.swift` | Core Data-backed repository implementation. `loadAllDaysWithUnreadable()` / `readAllDays()`: the day history that says what it could not read (an undecodable row's day named in `unreadableDayKeys`; not accounting for every row under read-only recovery, a failed day-row fetch or a row with no date key; a memo of a read that did not account for every row is never served — B3 fix rounds 1 and 2). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/Persistence.swift` | Core Data persistence controller setup for runtime and previews/tests. Owns `localDefaults`, the store-scoped device-local key/value surface (`UserDefaults.standard` for the default store, in-memory otherwise). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/StoreLocalDefaults.swift` | `StoreLocalDefaults` (the three `UserDefaults` accessors, which `UserDefaults` itself conforms to), `InMemoryStoreLocalDefaults` (a lock-guarded per-controller stand-in for in-memory and explicit-URL stores) and the shared `PendingResetBoundaryCoding` — the device-local, never-synced surface the ledgers keep their pending reset boundaries in, scoped exactly like the store so a test store never writes production defaults (tracker §3.6, 2026-09-24). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/StoreLocalDefaults+UserDefaults.swift` | The one-line `nonisolated extension UserDefaults: StoreLocalDefaults`, alone in its file on purpose: the persisted-surface wipe wall scans receiver-less accessor calls in any file declaring `extension UserDefaults`, and would read the protocol's own requirement declarations as unlabelled defaults writes. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/CoreDataModelBuilding.swift` | `CoreDataModelBuilding.makeAttribute` — the shared `NSAttributeDescription` factory for the two programmatic (no `.xcdatamodeld`) model builders, `PersistenceController` (CloudKitSync) and `PrivatePersistenceController` (PrivateStoreCore), each of which previously held a private byte-identical copy. Pure in-memory construction that names no sealed or synced entity, which is what lets it sit at layer 0 and be reused from both sides of the S3 wall. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/CloudKitDataService.swift` | CloudKit record read/write service and `ExistingDataSummary` detection used during onboarding migration; the sealed-backup record layout — the head under `sealed-backup.<payload>`, v1 suffix chunks `…chunk.<i>`, v2 suffix chunks scoped to their set `…chunk.<i>.<set>` (`saveSealedBackup(_:setTag:)`, `sealedBackupSuffixChunks`, `sealedBackupSuffixIsPresent` (the commit's verify, by record ID with no field or asset), `pruneSealedBackupSets` (never the set the current head names), `parseSealedBackupSuffixName`; the delete matcher knows both forms); `CloudKitRecordDatabase.existingRecordIDs` (a no-keys existence fetch). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/StoragePreferences.swift` | Codable model for user storage preferences: iCloud sync, local backup exclusion, HealthKit capability toggles, and sealed backup flags. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/PeriodTrackerStore.swift` | The cycle S3 funnel after the cutover (period-data design 2026-09-30, §6.3): the frozen cycle vocabulary, `PeriodLogOutcome` / `PeriodDeleteOutcome`, the Apple Health mirror seam (`PeriodHealthKitServicing`) and lock seam, and `PeriodTrackerStore` — save seals first and mirrors second, edit in place, delete Fernlet's rows before Health's copies, load with fill-on-read and dedupe, drain (v1 and v2 payloads), the Health-only day actions, the writer epoch. The held legacy-import task has no `deinit` cancel: `MemoryLifecycleBoundaryTests` (ML1) allowlists it with the invariant that makes that safe. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CycleDayEntry.swift` | `CycleDayEntry` (a day's records + Fernlet's Health copies with no record + other apps' samples, with derived accessors), `CycleHealthSamples` (the one rule turning Health samples into a clinical block / record), `FernletCycleRecordMirror` (the frozen `FernletCycleRecordID` metadata key) and `CycleLegacyIdentity` (deterministic legacy record ids). §6.4, §7.1, §8. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CycleLegacyImport.swift` | The legacy cycle import (§8): `CycleLegacyImportLedger` (the two persisted halves, set done by delete-all and reset) and `PeriodTrackerStore.runLegacyImportIfNeeded` — narratives and unmarked own Health samples into records in one merge write, rechecked after every await, the unopenable notes named for the Cycle page's card. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CycleMirrorDeletion.swift` | `CycleMirrorSampleKind` (the five Apple Health sample kinds a cycle mirror holds; in-memory, unfrozen; `kinds(writtenFor:)`, `possibleCopyKinds(of:)`) and `CycleMirrorDeletion` (a mirror delete's count and the kinds Apple Health refused — reported, not thrown, so a kind the entry never held neither stops a rewrite nor reads as "still in Apple Health"). Review round 1, R2. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/MenstrualNarrativeRepository.swift` | Persistence for menstrual narrative entries (the legacy source the cycle-record import retires). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CycleRecord.swift` | `CycleRecord` — one cycle entry sealed as ONE blob (day, clinical block, narrative block; each block nil = unknown), its frozen tolerant Codable (`"v": 2`), `CycleRecordOrigin`, the block types and the one merge rule `CycleRecord.merged` (whole blocks by clock; commutative, idempotent, associative; the origin follows the copy that speaks most for the clinical block, `combinedOrigin`). Period-data design 2026-09-30 §5.1. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CycleRecordRepository.swift` | Sealed at-rest CRUD for `CycleRecord` under `fernlet.cycle-record.v1`: the one `upsertMerged` write path (insert / merge / replace-dead / refuse-undecided, retiring legacy narratives in the same save), the edit, classified pages (dead on an id mismatch), keyless count/ids/deletes, bounded at 20 000 records. §5.2, §6.1. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CycleRecordStore.swift` | `@MainActor CycleRecordStore`: the fail-closed, visibility-gated funnel for every cycle-record read/write, the mutation hook and counter, and the backup seam (`backupChunk` / `restoreMerging` / `withBackupSeam`, plus `isStoreHealthy`; `backupPrePass` is not on the v2 engine's path). §6.2. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateStoreCore/PendingNarrativeBuffer.swift` | Codable buffer for pending menstrual narrative payloads (v1) and whole cycle records (v2, `cycleRecordJSON`); holds at most 200 and refuses the next with `.full` instead of evicting. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/SavedRecipe.swift` | Saved recipe model and repository implementations, including legacy JSON migration support. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/HealthKitGateway/ActivityTypeCatalog.swift` | Workout activity type search and HealthKit activity type mapping. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/FernletRepository.swift` | The abstract `FernletRepository` persistence contract for snapshot/day/tier-two load and save, and `loadAllDaysWithUnreadable()` → `DayHistoryRead` (`days`, the `unreadableDayKeys` of rows that would not decode, and `accountsForEveryRow` — false in read-only recovery, after a failed fetch or with a row that has no date key; the default is for stateless doubles only — B3 fix rounds 1 and 2). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/FernletSnapshot.swift` | `FernletSnapshot` persisted aggregate value type serialized to/from storage. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/HealthKitStorageStrip.swift` | The HealthKit half of the storage strip (HealthKit information is never stored in iCloud, 2026-09-23): `FernletDay.strippingHealthKitValues()` / `healthKitResidue` / `overlayingHealthKitResidue(_:)`, the device-local `DeviceHealthResidue` value, and the `DeviceHealthResidueStoring` cache contract (which since the body-profile follow-up also carries the imported body profile). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/DeviceHealthBodyProfile.swift` | `DeviceHealthBodyProfile` — the age, sex, height and weight THIS device imported from HealthKit, kept in the device cache instead of the synced `settings.userProfile` (2026-09-23): `applied(to:)` (the overlay, re-clamped), `removingFieldsEdited(from:to:)` and `typedProfile(_:editedFrom:to:)` (a profile-screen edit writes only the changed fields to settings). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/RemoteChangePublishingRepository.swift` | `RemoteChangePublishingRepository` refinement exposing an iCloud remote-change `AnyPublisher`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/DayRecordRepositoring.swift` | Per-day persistence protocol (`DayRecordRepositoring`) plus the sanitize-barrier `DayRecordUpsert` mint. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/SavedRecipeRepositoring.swift` | `@MainActor` append/upsert-only saved-recipe persistence protocol (`SavedRecipeRepositoring`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/CoinLedgerRepositoring.swift` | `@MainActor` append-only coin-ledger persistence protocol (`CoinLedgerRepositoring`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/CustomItemRepositoring.swift` | `@MainActor` append/upsert-only custom-item persistence protocol (`CustomItemRepositoring`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletPersistence/MilestoneLedgerRepositoring.swift` | `@MainActor` milestone-ledger persistence protocol (`MilestoneLedgerRepositoring`); append-only, deliberately no delete on the protocol — the wipe's `deleteAll()` lives only on the CloudKitSync conformer. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/DayContentSummary.swift` | `DayContentSummary` precomputed content-count roll-up carried in the aggregate blob for single-read existing-data detection. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/DerivedSignalFactory.swift` | `DerivedSignalFactory` computing the rolling derived-signal window (mood/energy/eating/progression/micronutrient trends) from stored day pairs. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/LogRecords.swift` | Derived-table DTO records (`DailyLogRecord`) persisted alongside the local database. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/TierTwoMemoryEngine.swift` | `TierTwoMemoryEngine` deriving capped tier-two behavioral memories from the rolling 14-day window. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/TierTwoMemoryStore.swift` | `TierTwoMemoryStore` — the device-local, never-synced, always-backup-excluded JSON sidecar the tier-two memories live in (owner decision 2026-09-23); refreshed by both repositories after a successful save, purged by delete everything. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/LocalPersistence/FeelingTagMoodScale.swift` | `FeelingTag.moodScore` — the fixed 0.2–1.0 mood scale shared by the deterministic derived engines, and the single home of the two previously duplicated private copies in `DerivedSignalFactory` and `TierTwoMemoryEngine`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/DayRecordRepository.swift` | Per-row Core Data + iCloud day-history store (`DayRecordRepository`) with duplicate-row dedup by `dateKey`; `loadAllWithUnreadable()` names the day of every row that would not decode and says whether every row was accounted for (the fetch succeeded, every row has its date key) — B3 fix rounds 1 and 2. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/RowPayloadCoders.swift` | `RowPayloadCoders`: single source of truth for the persisted-JSON encoder/decoder config (sorted keys + ISO-8601 dates, with opt-in pretty-printing for the human-readable on-disk files). Moved down to layer 0 from `CloudKitSync` so the local-only blob file in `LocalPersistence` shares it too; it replaced the per-store private `makeEncoder`/`makeDecoder` pairs that had drifted (the coin-ledger and custom-item row stores once wrote bare numeric dates). ISO-8601 truncates to whole seconds — callers comparing dates across representations must account for that. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/MultiDeviceSyncWarning.swift` | Pure `MultiDeviceSyncWarning` classifier for the "devices will diverge without iCloud" warning state. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/SealedBackupRecord.swift` | Sealed-backup transport DTOs (`SealedBackupRecord`, `SealedBackupPayloadType`): the opaque encrypted CloudKit record shape. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/SealedPhotoRecord.swift` | Own-photo escrow transport DTOs (`SealedPhotoCorpus`, `SealedPhotoSlot`, `SealedPhotoManifest`, `SealedPhotoRecord`): one encrypted record per photo id plus the sealed per-corpus manifest (id set + per-id content hash + the progress corpus's index sidecar). Born at record format 2 — no legacy shape to tolerate. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/DiaryStore/DiaryStore.swift` | `@MainActor @Observable DiaryStore`: the portable diary-state slice (scoring, meal/recipe/workout/journal/settings) carved from `FernletStore`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateStoreCore/PrivatePersistenceController.swift` | `PrivatePersistenceController`: dedicated local-only (never-iCloud) Core Data store for sealed, encrypted entities. Its model is versioned since 2026-09-30: V1 (the four shipped entities, frozen and checksum-pinned) and V2 (+ `CycleRecord`), with a V1 → V2 staged migration over in-memory model references on the store description. The bump is one-way: an earlier build opening a V2 store migrates it down and drops every `CycleRecord` row. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateStoreCore/PrivateRowPlumbing.swift` | `PrivateRowPlumbing.deleteRows` — the shared keyless fetch → guard → delete → save → history-prune sequence that the sealed repositories' `deleteAll()` methods (`JournalNarrativeRepository`, `WorryNarrativeRepository`, `IntimacyLogRepository`, `MenstrualNarrativeRepository`) each repeated inline. Rows are deleted without decrypting them, so deletion stays available while the app is locked or the feature is hidden, and the (rethrowing) persistent-history prune is part of the sequence. Deliberately not used by `purgeEncryptedEntities()`, whose wipe must stay atomic across every sealed entity under one save. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/IntimacyLogRepository.swift` | Sealed Core Data repository for `IntimacyLog` records (ChaChaPoly column-encrypted intimacy notes). Since unit B2 (Sealed backup v2, §8) it also carries the backup surface: the keyless id snapshot `allIDs()` (total order, bounded), the classified `logs(ids:contentKey:)` → `IntimacyLogPage`, the one restore write `upsertMerged(_:contentKey:)` → `IntimacyLogMergeResult` (insert absent ids with their own stamps and a capped note, keep openable rows and only fill a missing Health link, replace dead rows, never delete; one save, rolled back on any throw; idempotent), the keyless `delete(ids:)`, `isStoreHealthy` and `IntimacyLogRepositoryError`; `insertAtomically` is gone. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/IntimacyLogStore.swift` | `@MainActor IntimacyLogStore`: the fail-closed, visibility-gated funnel for every intimacy sealed-note read/write. Since unit B2: the mutation hook (`attachMutationHook`, run after an insert, a Health link, a merge that changed anything, a delete that removed rows) and the Sealed backup v2 seams — gated `backupChunk(ids:contentKey:)`, `restoreMerging(_:contentKey:)`, `withBackupSeam(_:)` (throw while hidden or keyless, never answer empty); ungated `allIDs()`, `isStoreHealthy`, `delete(ids:)`. `backupPage` and `restore` are gone. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateMemoryStore/JournalNarrativeRepository.swift` | Sealed journal-narrative store: `JournalNarrative` model, `JournalNarrativeStoring` protocol, and its encrypted repository. Its Sealed backup v2 surface (design 2026-09-30, §7, unit B3): `isStoreHealthy`, the keyless `allIDs()`, `ids(onDays:)` (every entry on the days whose day row will not decode, which the snapshot keeps — B3 fix round 2) and `skeletons(ids:)`, the classified `backupRecords(ids:hubKey:deviceKey:)` (`JournalBackupPage`; hub key, then the device key `JournalBackupDeviceKey`; an unknown tag needs a newer build, never dead) and THE restore write `upsertMerged(_:hubKey:deviceKey:)` (`JournalNarrativeMergeResult`: insert with the backup's stamps unless an entry on its day is already that entry — the same words and creation stamp, `JournalMergeDayEntry.isCopy(of:)`, B3 fix round 1 — keep an entry that opens, fork a different text unless an equal one is on its day, replace dead, never delete, idempotent; replaced `insertAtomically`); `apply` keeps a restored entry's stamps. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateMemoryStore/WorryNarrativeRepository.swift` | Sealed, device-only Worry Box store: `WorryNarrative` model, `WorryStoring` protocol, and its encrypted repository. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PeriodContextBridge/PeriodContextBridge.swift` | Period-module egress bridge: raw→abstract cycle conversions (`PeriodPhaseBand`) exporting only coarse enums past the S3 wall. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PeriodContextBridge/PeriodPhaseTrendEngine.swift` | `PeriodHealthTrend` device-sealed per-phase wellbeing trend (coarse direction + confidence band) from the user's own history. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/HeartDropCloudTransport.swift` | CloudKit **public**-database ferry for heart drops (the app's only public-DB use): upload one sealed drop per tag, fetch a friend's tag window with per-chunk anti-starvation budgeting. Sees tags and ciphertext only. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedBackupGenerationStore.swift` | Device-local, never-synced high-water mark of the highest sealed-backup generation this device has written or accepted, per payload type **and per own-photo corpus** (a separate key namespace, since the two routes have independent write histories) — the state half of the rollback defense. The crypto half is the generation binding in `SealedBackupCrypto.authenticatedData`. `mintNext` (persist-before-upload) is the v1 exports'; a v2 set's generation is recorded through `recordAccepted` only on a verified commit or an accepted restore. Cleared by the delete-all path (the v2 accepted heads are not in it and are kept). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/CloudKitSync/CloudKitSchemaDeploy.swift` | The schema-deploy launch flag. `isRequested(arguments:)` is pure and testable; the caller that actually pushes the schema is `#if DEBUG`-gated so it cannot fire in Release. See [CloudKit-Schema-Deploy.md](CloudKit-Schema-Deploy.md). |

## Domain Models (FernletDomainModel)

The portable value-type layer that replaced the app's old `Models.swift`, split per area.

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/WellbeingModels.swift` | Day, health-context, journal, sleep, hygiene, goals, and daily-score value models (`FernletDay`, …). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/NutritionModels.swift` | Nutrition, food, meal, recipe, and macro/micronutrient value models (`UserNutritionProfile`, …). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FoodPortionReader.swift` | The tolerant USDA portion reader (ingredient-search round F4a): a portion's leading measure word decides ("cup, sliced" is a cup, "medium (7\" long)" a size, "clove" a count noun; frozen English tokens) and `eachPortion(in:)` picks what "1 each" means — the medium among several sizes, the single named count, or the one its NLEA/RACC serving names. A count unit on a USDA yield ("piece … (yield from 1 lb raw meat)") and a named count holding a part or packaging word ("apricot half", "small box") are not read (fix round 1). Also `FoodPortion.recipeUnit` (exact reading first). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeHouseholdMeasure.swift` | How a household recipe amount is saved (F4a): "1 each" of a banana becomes `118 g` plus the additive optional display metadata `householdMeasure` ("medium", 118 g per one) — grams every build and peer resolves; re-opened as "1 each" in the editor and shown as "1 medium (118 g)". A line an older build already reads the same ("1 cup" of a food stating one cup) is kept as typed; applied by the editor, a substitution fork and a meal-log recipe (`RecipeDefinition.savingHouseholdAsGrams`) (fix round 1). F4b adds `labelHead`/`labelQualifier`/`headTakesPlural` (frozen `pluralNouns`), so a caption's noun agrees with its count ("2 eggs", "2 cups, sliced", never "2 mediums"). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipePortionPicker.swift` | The recipe editor's per-food unit menu (ingredient-search round F4b): `RecipePortionOption` (a unit, one of the food's USDA portions, a USDA typical size or the person's own size) and `RecipePortionPicker.choices(for:personal:)` — the food's own portions first as what one is with its grams ("1 medium (118 g)", "1 cup, sliced (150 g)"; `FoodPortionReader.householdLabel(of:)`), then personal sizes, then typical sizes where the food states none of that kind, then only the units that convert; a unit whose one IS a listed portion is folded into it (`standIns`). Also the amount carried across a pick and how a saved line re-opens (`reopened`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/TypicalPortionTable.swift` | Curated USDA typical sizes (F4b, report §6.3 Rungs C/D): 76 frozen English keys — produce, eggs, chicken and butter counts, baking and pantry cups and spoons — every weight cited to the SR Legacy row and portion text it is read from (verified against the shipped catalog). A row IS an entry's ingredient by its F5 head noun, its key words and none of its excluded (processed-form) words; offered only where the food's own data has none of that kind, badged as an estimate; spoons derive from a stated cup's density. Read by the recipe editor and the web importer's fallback, never by the converter. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/WorkoutModels.swift` | Workout, exercise, muscle, and equipment value models (`Workout`, …). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/WorkoutProgram.swift` | Workout program/training model value types: `ExperienceLevel`, `WorkoutProfile`, training-split selection. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/WorkoutRestGuidance.swift` | Evidence-based default rest-between-sets table (`WorkoutRestGuidance`) keyed by movement `Demand` and training goal. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/CompanionModels.swift` | Companion appearance, workshop, and texture value models (`WorkshopData`, `TextureEntry`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/CompanionEmotion.swift` | `CompanionEmotion` — the companion's momentary feeling (11 FROZEN tokens + display forks + "Okay, feeling sleepy"); deliberately not `Codable`; `isWidgetPublishable` keeps five in the app. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/SettingsModel.swift` | `FernletSettings` app-settings serialization aggregate (hydration, companion, equipped items, designer provenance). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/AgeAssurance.swift` | `AgeGate` (13/16/18), `AgeGateVerdict`, `AgeAssuranceProvenance`, and `AgeAssuranceRecord` — the pure age-gate rules: fail-closed, a below-gate verdict is unappealable, and provenance is asymmetric (it can close a gate but not open one). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/NavigationEnums.swift` | Screen/widget/shortcut navigation enums (`FernletScreen`, `ConnectionInspectorMode`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ScoringValueTypes.swift` | Pure scoring value types (`ScoringWeights`) carved out of the app-layer scoring logic. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FoodItemSearch.swift` | Pure relevance-search value logic over `FoodItem` plus a restaurant-chain brand lexicon (`FoodItemSearch`, `FoodBrandLexicon`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FoodIngredientIdentity.swift` | The recipe surfaces' ranking (ingredient-search round F5): `FoodSearchRanking` (`.standard` everywhere by default, `.ingredientIdentity` for the recipe editor's typeahead and the swap sheet) and `FoodIngredientIdentity`, which decides from a row's own name whether it IS the typed ingredient — the head noun of the first comma segment, plural-aware; a USDA class's member ("Cheese, parmesan", "Squash, zucchini"), read from the third segment when the second only qualifies the class ("Squash, summer, zucchini", "Chicken, broilers or fryers, thigh"); a phrase ending at a preposition; coordination; part nouns ("Ginger root"); one-word parenthetical synonyms ("(cilantro)") — so "Cookies, chocolate chip" and "Bread, banana" never count. A product name is one phrase (a preposition makes it a composite; a first segment followed by anything but an echo of its words or a variant word — "Salsa, Mild", "Chocolate Chips, Semi-sweet" — is a flavor list, so "Lemon, Ginger Drink" is no lemon); a person's own food is read by its first segment ("Chicken breast, grilled"). Two levels: a USDA reference row that writes a typed modifier as its kind ("Milk, whole" for "whole milk") leads the other USDA rows — read after history and source, so a person's own and logged foods still lead. Off while the head is still being typed (a trailing single letter, or under one ranked row in ten saying it whole). Frozen English word lists. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FoodSearchHistory.swift` | Research §26 fix 1.9's history-first ranking input (`FoodSearchHistory`): the foods this user has logged, weighted `log(1 + count)` × exponential recency decay (τ = 21 days), read as the TOP key of `FoodItemSearch`'s comparator on the typed-query surface only. DERIVED from `DiaryStore.recentMeals` on every write and at init — no new persisted surface, so the diary's own wipe is the feature's wipe. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeSourceURLMatcher.swift` | The one source-URL normalization rule (scheme/host case-insensitive, fragment stripped, query kept) behind the zero-network duplicate skip, the supersede-on-re-import match, the superseded-photo cleanup, and the mesh already-saved decision. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/EnumDecodeCompat.swift` | Forward-compatible decode helpers (`EnumDecodeCompat`) for synced raw-value enums: park and re-adopt unknown tokens via side channels. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/AIAnalysisRetryRecord.swift` | Codable retry-queue record (`AIAnalysisRetryRecord`) for pending AI-analysis payloads; element type of `FernletSnapshot.retryQueue`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/AIDestination.swift` | `AIDestination` enum naming where an AI call is routed (on-device FoundationModels or web nutrition lookup). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/TierTwoMemoryRecord.swift` | Codable tier-two behavioral memory record (`TierTwoMemoryRecord`) referenced by the repository and memory agent. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/DiagnosticLanguage.swift` | Pure best-effort classifier (`DiagnosticLanguage`) flagging clinical/diagnostic language to screen out of AI prompts and stored memories. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/JournalMemoryCapture.swift` | Core Memory's journal capture (owner decision 2026-09-23): `MemoryNote.emotionOnly(for:)` mints a journal entry's memory as its `FeelingTag` token with NO text, and `JournalMemorySummaryPolicy` is the test an AI summary must pass to become that memory's text (bounded, no diagnostic language, not verbatim / a prefix / an excerpt). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/CustomItemModels.swift` | User-designed custom clothing/accessory models: `ItemSlot` taxonomy, palette-indexed grid textures, `CustomizationItem`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ItemDesigner.swift` | `ItemDesigner` provenance value carrying only an anonymous, stable designer id for "designed by …" attribution. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ItemNameModeration.swift` | Pure profanity/slur name gate (`ItemNameModeration`) for items listed for sale in the clothing shop. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ClothingModeration.swift` | Value types and pure verdict math for the in-person clothing-shop report/ban moderation ledger (`ReportReason`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ModerationBanRecovery.swift` | `BanEvidence` + `ModerationBanRecovery` — the pure half of lifting a store ban when its reporters withdraw (2026-09-24): which recorded evidence a `retract` positively withdrew (absence never counts), the bounded canonical merge, and the monotone uncapped threshold test. Reporter identities arrive as opaque tags through a closure, so the module stays crypto-free. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ClothingShopLimits.swift` | Shared constants and a wire-boundary sanitizer (`ClothingShopLimits`) clamping untrusted peer-received shop items. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/CoinEconomy.swift` | Append-only coin-ledger model (`CoinLedgerEntry`, `CoinLedgerKind` earn/spend) and balance-aggregation math with structural idempotency. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/MilestoneLedger.swift` | Append-only cumulative care-milestone ledger model (`MilestoneEventKind`, `MilestoneLedgerEntry`); lifetime counts, monotonic between wipes (since 2026-08-20 "delete everything" clears the ledger, reversing the earlier survive-reset rule; since 2026-08-21 the wipe also appends a `resetBoundary` marker and aggregation counts only rows whose day is at/after the marker's day AND created strictly after its instant, so neither re-synced pre-wipe rows nor rows re-derived from re-synced days can resurrect the trail). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/IdentityDedup.swift` | `Array.deduplicatedByID()` — the application-level union-merge that collapses duplicate-id rows (first seen wins, order preserved), needed because `NSPersistentCloudKitContainer` mirrors by record identity and does not honor an id attribute as unique. The one implementation now behind `CoinEconomy.deduplicatedByID`, `MilestoneEconomy.deduplicatedByID`, and the StoreCore per-row services' load paths, which each held their own copy of the loop. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/Closeness.swift` | Deterministic closeness math and close-friend slot assignment: `FriendInteractionDayCounts` and capped daily warmth points. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FriendState.swift` | Friend-facing fuzzy wellbeing state (`FriendFuzzyState`) and its sealed shareable payload, derived from the state enum, never the score. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/HeartSharing.swift` | Wire model (`HeartPayload`) and presentation math for proximity "send good vibes" hearts between friends. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ActivityModels.swift` | Pure value types for proximity group activities: host-signed versioned rosters, join tokens, and `ActivityLimits`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ProximityCoordinatorEnums.swift` | Top-level proximity enums (`ProximityRole`, `ProximityMode`, `ProximityRangingMode`) hoisted out of `ProximityCoordinator`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ProximityPersistenceRecords.swift` | Codable proximity trust/audit DTOs (`ProximityTrustedPeerRecord`) for the persisted, synced trust vault. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FDADailyValues.swift` | The single FDA Daily Value reference table shared by the gap analyzer and the label scanner, verified against 21 CFR 101.9. Flat label-comparison reference — no age/sex adjustment, no upper intake levels. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/GroceryAggregation.swift` | Pure, catalog-free shopping-list aggregation (F3, §11.3): merges several recipes' ingredients into one consolidated list. Below the wall, no I/O — `FoodItem` resolution stays the caller's job. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeScaling.swift` | Pure proportional recipe scaling (F4, §11.4). A non-persisted view/share-time transform returning scaled *copies* — it never mutates a stored recipe, resolves no `FoodItem`, and touches no catalog. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeSubstitution.swift` | A bound substitution suggestion: a catalog `FoodItem` proposed to replace one ingredient, plus an optional display-only reason. The model picks a candidate *number*; code binds it back to the catalog. A fork of a multipart recipe keeps its parts, the substitute re-pointed into the replaced row's part. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeComponents.swift` | Multipart recipes (2026-09-24): `RecipeComponent`, an id PARTITION over a recipe's flat `ingredients`/`steps` (never a copy, so nutrition, scaling, grocery and old readers count every row once), and `RecipeDefinition.resolvedComponents`, the one resolution rule (unclaimed rows join the last part, empty parts drop, fewer than two collapse to one, at most 12). Also `RecipeComponentNaming`, the editor's `RecipeComponentInput` + `RecipeComponentAssembly` (parts → rows + partition through the one-part upsert/sanitizer), and `cookingSteps` (making order with part names). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeComponentWire.swift` | The multipart half of the `fernlet.recipe` wire: version stays 1, the flat arrays carry the whole recipe with section-labelled steps (`RecipeComponentWire`, label token ": "), and the optional `components` partition (`SharedRecipeComponent`, strict decode, `isValidPartition`) lets a newer reader rebuild the parts (`componentSlices`, `RecipeComponentImport.layout`) while an older one reads a flat recipe. `assembled`, `droppingComponents` (the old-reader form) and `withoutSteps` (the notes toggle) live here. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/HeartDropTransport.swift` | The `HeartDropRecord` (rotating pairwise day tag + sealed blob) and the `HeartDropTransporting` seam. The transport neither knows nor can learn who either endpoint is — this is why the wall holds across CloudKit. |

## Food, Nutrition, And Recipe Services

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/FoodDataCatalog.swift` | USDA food item record decoder (compact + raw FDC), source-JSON loading for the DB generator, `FoodItemSearch` scorer, and recipe search helpers. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/FoodCatalog.swift` | `FoodCatalog` service: merges the SQLite bundled food source with the in-memory user-item snapshot; search via FTS candidate prefilter → `FoodItemSearch` scorer, plus ID/exact-name resolution. Replaces the old in-memory `allFoodItems` array. Also holds the fix-1.10 correction-alias snapshot (`setSearchAliases`), which promotes the user's own remembered pick to rank 1 on `results(for:)` — never on the bind-confidence `scoredResults`. Since F9b also the recipe-pick snapshot (`setRecipeSearchPicks`), answered only by `.ingredientIdentity` searches; on that surface `candidates(for:limit:ranking:)` puts the remembered answer for the WHOLE description first, exempt from the dish demotion (F9b fix round 1 — the sub-phrases never spell "2% milk" or a fourth word), while the resolver's `.standard` pool keeps the per-sub-phrase promotion only. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/BundledFoodStore.swift` | `BundledFoodSource` protocol + read-only SQLite-backed `SQLiteBundledFoodSource` (FTS5 candidate query, id/exact lookups, hydration) and `InMemoryBundledFoodSource` for tests; shared `FoodCatalogSchema`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/BundledRowCorrection.swift` | Load-time corrections every hydrated SQLite row passes through, fixing defects in the committed catalog without regenerating it: the branded per-100 g basis rebase (F2, a 100 g serving plus the label serving as an `each` portion) and the raw FDC serving-unit aliases (F1(a), `GRM`/`GM` → `g`, `MLT` → `ml`), and the retype of packaged products filed as SR Legacy (F6: by category, plus ten filed under an SR food group, by frozen FDC id). Pure, per-row, O(1). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/BundledFoodSupplement.swift` | `BundledFoodSupplement` (reads `Resources/FoodCatalogSupplement.json`, the 26 zero-energy SR Legacy foods the catalog build dropped) and `SupplementedBundledFoodSource`, which serves the base SQLite catalog plus those rows as one bundled source (FTS-equivalent prefix-AND gate, id/batch/exact lookups). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/TypeaheadDuplicateCollapse.swift` | One row per catalog name (within a data type) in a typed search: keeps the physically plausible row whose tap default converts, at the group's first position; never touches user items or logged rows. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/CuratedSearchAlias.swift` | The curated typed-search alias table (ingredient-search round F7): frozen English phrases ("chocolate chip(s)", "garlic clove(s)", "red pepper flakes", "vegetable oil") naming the USDA row whose name does not say them, by catalog id; prefix-aware on the last word, "semi sweet" folded to "semisweet". Typed search only — inserted beneath a correction and this person's own/logged rows, never reaching the resolver pool, the importer's bind or a bind score. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/Resources/FoodCatalogSupplement.json` | The 26 zero-energy SR Legacy foods (salt, baking soda, waters, teas) served beside `FoodCatalog.sqlite`, in the compact source schema. Generated — edit only via `Scripts/food-catalog/sr_zero_energy_supplement.py`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodCatalogDatabaseBuilder.swift` | Build-time generator that converts the `FoodDataSource/*.json` foods into the shipped read-only `FoodCatalog.sqlite` (run via the gated `FoodCatalogGenerationTests`). Not used at runtime. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/food-catalog/` | Offline, standard-library preservation pipeline for the approved FDC/FNDDS/curated-branded/CoFID source set; builds only ignored validation artifacts and never replaces the shipping catalog. Its README is the reproducibility and corrective-workflow contract. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/food-catalog/sr_zero_energy_supplement.py` | Not part of that pipeline: regenerates `FoodCatalog/Resources/FoodCatalogSupplement.json` by rule from USDA's SR Legacy 2018-04 JSON (manifest-verified size and SHA-256) — the zero-energy SR foods the committed catalog's build dropped — after proving its field mapping reproduces all 7,761 SR rows the catalog kept. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/food-catalog/misfiled_branded_audit.py` | Not part of that pipeline either: proves FoodCatalog's load-time retype of packaged products filed as SR Legacy (`BundledRowCorrection`, both rules read out of the Swift source) picks exactly the rows FDC's own `food.csv` types `branded_food` — 773 = 763 by category + 10 by FDC id — and leaves only `sr_legacy_food`/`foundation_food` rows `srLegacy`. Reads the manifest-pinned 2026-04-30 FDC CSV archive (size, SHA-256 and the `food.csv` member hash checked); exits non-zero on any disagreement. |
| `Fernlet/FoodDataSource/FoodCatalogSourceManifest.json` | Immutable source/version/hash/license/attribution contract, strict FDC allowlist, and explicit CNF/NEVO exclusions for the offline preservation pipeline. |
| `Fernlet/FoodDataSource/FoodCatalogGenerationEvidence.json` | Compact measured validation, FTS, preservation, size/ODR arithmetic, and committed-catalog checksum evidence for the historical schema-v3 local generation; current schema-v4 reproducibility results are recorded in the round ledger. Contains no raw data or SQLite payload. |
| `Fernlet/Docs/Food-Catalog-Runtime-ODR-Options-2026-08-24.md` | Measured base/ODR/preservation distribution options, P0/P1/P2 projection boundary, and the owner decision required before any runtime catalog or ODR integration. |
| `Fernlet/FoodDataSource/USDAFoodItems.json` | Source USDA food item dataset consumed by the build-time DB generator (not bundled at runtime). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/Resources/FoodCatalog.sqlite` | The shipped read-only SQLite food catalog (FTS5), generated from `FoodDataSource/` and loaded via `Bundle.module`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutExercises.json` | Bundled exercise dataset consumed by the workout system. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/FoundationFoodSelection.swift` | Foundation model availability and food selection model helpers. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/NutritionLabelScanner.swift` | Nutrition label scan result model, scan errors, and scanner implementation. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/NutritionLabelCameraSheet.swift` | Camera and image picker UI for capturing nutrition labels and reviewing scan results. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/RecipeWebImporter.swift` | Recipe import pipeline for extracting structured recipes from web content and metadata. With no nutrition label it estimates macros from the ingredient lines against the USDA catalog: units read longest first and must end where they end, count/size words bind "each" only on a row with a count portion, and a line that cannot be counted is skipped and counted (`ImportedRecipe.uncountedIngredientCount`) rather than voiding the page (ingredient-search round F11); Unicode fractions ("½", "1½") fold to ASCII first, a bare count ("1 lemon") counts one serving only where the bound row's serving is one item (never SR's 100 g reference amount), and a line that opens with an amount but cannot be read past it counts as left out. F4b: a line the bound row's own data cannot weigh is weighed by the USDA typical size of the ingredient that row IS (`TypicalPortionTable`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeShareCodec.swift` | The `fernlet.recipe` v1 payload: builds it for the proximity mesh (`proximityPayload`, the `.local` arm carrying a multipart recipe's `components` partition; older builds read the flattened recipe), and reads pasted text (`decodePayload`). Since 2026-09-30 it no longer WRITES the paste text — the share sheet sends `RecipeShareText` — but still reads the text older builds shared (the frozen `legacyPayloadMarker`, "Fernlet recipe data:") and the bare JSON the Shortcuts file import hands it. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeShareText.swift` | `RecipeShareText` (2026-09-30) — the readable text "Share as text" hands the system share sheet: name, servings, one per-serving macro line (calories only when shown), ingredients (under each part's name, with the recipe page's amounts), numbered steps, notes and a web recipe's lines, estimate note and source. No JSON and no Fernlet data (the owner's report). Labels localize in the sender's language; recipe content stays verbatim. "Include notes" off withholds the notes and — for a recipe the person made — the steps, as the nearby share does. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeShareTextItemSource.swift` | `RecipeShareTextItemSource` (2026-09-30) — the `UIActivityItemSource` "Share as text" hands `ActivityShareView`: every activity gets the readable string, and the recipe's name is the sheet's title (a local, title-only `LPLinkMetadata` — no URL, no fetch) and Mail's subject. Chosen over `ShareLink`, whose string form leaves the title blank and whose `SharePreview` form drops Copy for Save to Files (measured on the iOS 26.5 simulator). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeMessageComposer.swift` | `RecipeMessageComposer` + `RecipeMessagesSendOutcome` (2026-09-30) — the `MFMessageComposeViewController` behind "Send in Messages", holding only the Fernlet recipe card (`FernletMessagesCard`); the delegate hops to the main actor. `canSendText` is read when the row is drawn and again at the tap, because presenting a composer where Messages is not set up (every simulator) throws. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealBuilder.swift` | Converts a `FoodSelectionPlan` and food candidates into structured `Meal` records and inline recipe definitions, with good-protein threshold logic. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/CustomIngredientUpsert.swift` | Resolves manual recipe ingredient inputs into `FoodItem` records, creating or updating custom food entries in the catalog. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/PreciseMacros.swift` | `PreciseMacros` — decimal ingredient grams ("3.4 g protein", 2026-09-29), the side channel beside whole-gram `Macros`: a sanitizing init, `rounded` by the same rule as `Macros.scaled(by:)`, and `scaled(by:)` mirroring it exactly, so whole-gram foods keep every number. `roundedToTenths` / `scaledToTenths(by:)` are the tenth-of-a-gram precision a decimal food is stored, scaled and shown at, so a row's "2.5 g" always counts 3 g. Stored on `FoodItem.preciseMacros` and the `fernlet.recipe` wire's optional `preciseMacros` key; never in the hash-covered exchange packet. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/MacroGramEntry.swift` | `MacroGramEntry` — the typed-grams rule for macro fields: parses either decimal separator through `LocaleTolerantNumber`, rejects negatives and garbage, stores tenths of a gram, and displays in the locale's separator with no grouping and no ".0", so a prefill always parses back. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeQuantityDisplay.swift` | `RecipeQuantityDisplay` — an ingredient quantity or serving size for a row that also shows `MacroGramEntry` grams (the recipe editor's collapsed ingredient row, the catalog suggestion row): up to two decimal places in the locale's separator, never grouped, so "1,5 cup · P3,4g" never mixes separators the way `%g` did. Display only. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealResolutionService.swift` | `MealResolutionService` + `MealPlausibility` — the quick-log meal-resolution cascade (cold whole-description probe → AI decomposition → AI selection → lexicon → plan → heuristic) plus the micronutrient fallback and implausible-total gate. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WholeDescriptionFoodProbe.swift` | Cold pre-decomposition whole-description resolver probe: requires the corpus-calibrated high score floor plus a typed-unit `FoodPortion`, computes its grams, emits only a validated food-serving basis, and conservatively surfaces stripped brand/retailer text. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoundationDishDecomposition.swift` | `FoundationDishDecompositionModel` — on-device Foundation Models decomposition of a meal description into catalog-resolved, scaled `Meal` components with a match confidence. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DishTemplateLexicon.swift` | `DishTemplateLexicon` + JSON template types — loads `DishTemplates.json` for deterministic, count-aware dish-template lookup (the decomposition fallback path). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodCaptureRouter.swift` | `FoodCaptureRoute` + router — auto-detects a captured photo (barcode / nutrition label / meal) and routes to the matching existing flow, or `.ambiguous` for a gentle chooser. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealPhotoRecognizer.swift` | `MealPhotoRecognizer` (+ host protocol) — on-device Vision food-photo classification that composes a text description into the existing `resolveMeals` cascade, always pausing at review. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealPhotoPolaroid.swift` | `MealPhotoPolaroid` — polaroid-framed meal-photo tile with lazy sealed-byte load and `MealPhotoPresence` classification (here / on other device / unreadable). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PhotoSaveFailureAcknowledgement.swift` | `PhotoSaveFailureAcknowledgement` — the session-photo review hosts' one-shot wait for a failed "Also save kept photos to Photos" alert to be closed before the review leaves the session and hides (design §4.6's order: commit, export, the alert inside the review, then the leave and the hide). Resumed when the failure binding clears or the sheet disappears, so a torn-down sheet never strands the answer. Since U3 its two hosts are `SessionPhotoReviewCoordinator` (the overlay, resumed by `SessionPhotoReviewScreen`) and the camera's Develop sheet. **U3 fix round 1 (U3-C-U3-R1):** it waits only while the review that shows the alert is OPEN — `reviewDidOpen()` / `reviewDidClose()` (a close also resumes a waiter), starting closed; a wait begun after the review went returns at once and audits `sessionPhotoReview.exportFailureUnseen` (context `host`), because that wait never ended and held the discovery block's answer leg for the process. Both instances live on the coordinator (`host` "overlay" / "develop"). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecentBites.swift` | `RecentBite` + `MealPhotoPresence` — pure 7-day windowing behind Home's "Recent bites" photo strip (resolves which photographed meals to show without reading sealed bytes). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BarcodeScanView.swift` | `BarcodeResolveFlowView` / `BarcodeScanView` — scan a product barcode and resolve it (user items → catalog → gentle not-found that creates a food item with the barcode remembered). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BrandedCatalogResourceLoader.swift` | `BrandedCatalogResourceLoader` — loads the ~364k-product branded food DB (`FoodCatalogBranded.sqlite`) via bundle-first or On-Demand Resource and attaches it as a secondary `FoodCatalog` source. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodProductWebImporter.swift` | `FoodProductWebImporter` / `FoodProductWebSearch` / `ImportedFoodProduct` — on-device Foundation Models import of a branded product's nutrition from a web product page. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OpenFoodFactsClient.swift` | `OpenFoodFactsClient` — the optional online UPC lookup's ONE network seam: a read-only `GET` to `world.openfoodfacts.org` (API v3.4) through `EphemeralWebSession`, redirects refused, 128 KB cap, 20 s deadline, consent re-checked inside. A no-tracking-wall pinned HTTP-client file (see [No-Tracking-Wall.md](No-Tracking-Wall.md) §3). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OpenFoodFactsProduct.swift` | The pure half of the Open Food Facts lookup: `OpenFoodFactsBarcode` (GTIN validation, UPC-E expansion, OFF's code normalization), the bounded response classifier and product parser, and the mapping into a `.openFoodFacts` `FoodItem` / the naming screen's scan model. No network API named. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OpenFoodFactsLookupCard.swift` | The lookup's consent gate (`OpenFoodFactsLookupAccess`), audited run (`OpenFoodFactsBarcodeLookup` — AI activity log at dispatch), `FernletStore.saveOpenFoodFactsFood`, and the not-found screen's one-tap-per-lookup card carrying the ODbL attribution. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/NutritionTargetsEditor.swift` | `NutritionTargetsEditor` — Settings card to edit macro targets (calories/protein/fat overridable, carbs shown as the live rebalancing residual); carries `WeightManagementDeficitRow` above Calories while Weight Management is the goal. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WeightManagementDeficitRow.swift` | `WeightManagementDeficitRow` + its view-free rules `WeightManagementDeficitControl` — the Weight Management calorie-deficit stepper on the Nutrition targets card (0–20% in 5% steps, 10% by default, owner 2026-09-24), shown only for that goal, disabled with the reason while a pinned calorie target outranks it. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/Scoring.swift` | Health scoring, goal weights, meal parsing, workout planning, workout suggestions, and date helpers. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/CompanionEmotionEngine.swift` | `CompanionEmotionEngine` — the pure emotion derivation and its explicit precedence table, `CompanionEmotionInputs` (+ `widgetSafe`, `isGentleDay`), `CompanionSleepWindow`, and the time-stable day timeline the widget renders from. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/PeriodScoringSignals.swift` | Abstract period-scoring vocabulary (`PeriodSignalStrength`, `PeriodPhaseSignal`, `PeriodScoringAdjustment`) consumed by the scoring engine. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/BarcodeScanner.swift` | Vision still-photo product-barcode detection (`BarcodePayloadDetecting` seam + `VisionBarcodeDetector`/`BarcodeScanner`) over EAN-13/EAN-8/UPC-E, the fallback for devices lacking live `DataScannerViewController`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/FoodImageClassifier.swift` | On-device meal-photo image classification behind the `FoodImageClassifying` seam (`VisionFoodImageClassifier` via `VNClassifyImageRequest`), returning raw taxonomy labels; the photo never leaves the device. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/WebScrapingKit/EphemeralWebSession.swift` | `EphemeralWebSession` — the single private-browsing `URLSession` (ephemeral + cookies/cache/credentials explicitly disabled) that both web importers and the Open Food Facts barcode client fetch through. The per-setting rationale, including which knobs are redundant under `.ephemeral` and why they are set anyway, lives here (see [No-Tracking-Wall.md](No-Tracking-Wall.md) §2a). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/WebScrapingKit/HTMLScraper.swift` | `HTMLScraper` — shared regex capture reads, HTML-entity decoding (`decodingNumericEntities:` preserves both importers' differing policies), and the body-to-plain-text pass; returns `nil` on an empty page so each caller throws its own error. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/WebScrapingKit/JSONLDScraper.swift` | `JSONLDScraper` — `application/ld+json` script extraction, `@type` reading, and the recursive `@graph`/`itemListElement` search that finds a schema.org `Product` or `Recipe`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/CuratedNutrientSources.swift` | Hand-authored "good source" foods per tracked micronutrient — the F2 gap-filling nudge's payload, pinned to real bundled-catalog ids so the card can bind the food and offer "add it". |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GroceryListComposer.swift` | F3 app-target glue between the recipe stores and the pure `GroceryAggregation` engine: catalog resolution the engine refuses to do, F4 "cook for N" scaling, and web-import lines through `DataExportBuilder.recipeIngredientLines`. The list is one-shot share text; only the weekly plan persists. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GroceryPlannerView.swift` | The Phase A shopping-list builder: multi-select recipes with an optional per-recipe yield, preview the aggregated list, share as plain text. Nothing here persists. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/IngredientSubstitutionSheet.swift` | F4 substitution UI (§11.4): replace ONE structured ingredient. Applying forks a new recipe only on an explicit "Save as new recipe" tap — cancelling forks nothing and never mutates the source. That tap also remembers a replacement picked from below the top of the manual search list for words the person typed (ingredient-search round F9b, `RecipeSearchPick`) — never an AI suggestion, the seeded search or a search for the ingredient being replaced, and (fix round 1) never a row that does not say the words searched for while another row does: the list pools each sub-phrase's rows, so "Coconut" from the "coconut" block is a stand-in, not what "coconut oil" means. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CookingMode.swift` | `CookingModeAvailability` — the single rule deciding whether a recipe gets a "Cook" action (authored steps, structured ingredients, or a web import's free-text lines). UIKit-free. The mise en place lays a multipart recipe out part by part ("Made in 2 parts. Start with …") and the walker names the current part. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePartsDraft.swift` | The recipe editor's multipart builder logic with no SwiftUI (`RecipePartsDraft`: split, add/remove/move parts, per-part rows and steps, whole-recipe caps, collapse back to one part), and `RecipeEditorInputs` (stored rows → editor rows, shared by the one-part editor and every part). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePartsEditor.swift` | The editor's parts UI: `RecipePartsEditor` (one `RecipePartEditorCard` per part, "Add another part") plus the row and step editors now SHARED with the one-part editor (`RecipeIngredientRows`, `RecipeStepEditorCard`, `RecipeEditorActionLabel`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePartsViews.swift` | Read-side part grouping for the recipe detail, mise en place and the proximity review sheet: `RecipePartsLayout` (displayed/scaled rows grouped by id), `RecipePartHeader` ("Part 1 of 2", a VoiceOver heading), `RecipePartsIngredientList`, `RecipePartsStepList`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CookingLiveActivityController.swift` | App-target-only requester of the cooking Live Activity (starting one is the only thing solely the app process can do). Updates/ends go through the shared `CookingActivityBridge`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BarcodeServingStepView.swift` | The quick serving-count step after a barcode scan, plus its device-local last-serving memory keyed by normalized GTIN — a `UserDefaults` sidecar deliberately kept off the synced/sealed blob path. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeWebImageAttemptMemory.swift` | Device-local `UserDefaults` sidecar recording which recipes' one automatic web-image download THIS device already attempted — the per-device half of the “one attempt per device, suppression syncs” contract (the synced half is `RecipeWebImport.webImageSuppressed`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodSearchCorrectionMemory.swift` | Research §26 fix 1.10's local correction memory: a device-local `UserDefaults` sidecar (never synced between devices, capped at 200) pairing the text a user searched in "Adjust meal" with the food they picked, republished into `FoodCatalog` so the same search self-heals thereafter. Since F9b each entry carries an origin (frozen `correction` / `recipePick`; a correction's stored bytes are unchanged): recipe picks publish into `FoodCatalog.setRecipeSearchPicks` (recipe surfaces only), an ordinary pick never overwrites a correction while a correction replaces a pick, and the cap spends picks before corrections. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeSearchPick.swift` | Ingredient-search round F9b: when a pick in a recipe ingredient list teaches search — a row below the first, a real search, a finished last word (plural-aware; "chocolate ch" teaches nothing), and in the swap sheet not the seeded search or a search for the ingredient being replaced, and only a row that says the words the swap list searched for (its single sub-phrase words, either number) unless no shown row does (fix round 1) — and the recipe picks a save's rows carry (`ManualRecipeIngredientInput.pickedForSearch`). The recipe editor's tap only marks the row; the store's recipe saves write. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePortionGramsMemory.swift` | F4b's "grams in one" memory: a device-local `UserDefaults` sidecar (`fernlet.recipePortionGrams.v1`, never synced, capped at 200, oldest-out) of the grams a person gave for one of a food in place of a USDA typical size, written when a recipe is saved and offered under "Your size" next time; cleared by the wipe funnel. |
