## Scripts And CI

| Path | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/spm-wall-check.sh` | The ONE strict build: `DIAGNOSE_MISSING_TARGET_DEPENDENCIES=YES_ERROR` (S3 wall — a forbidden cross-wall `import` fails the build) plus `SUPPRESS_WARNINGS=NO SWIFT_TREAT_WARNINGS_AS_ERRORS=YES GCC_TREAT_WARNINGS_AS_ERRORS=YES` (Power-of-10 rule 10 — every warning, including the package warnings Xcode otherwise hides, is an error). Run pre-merge and in CI. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/power-of-10-scan.py` | Power-of-10 mechanical scan (rules 2/4/5/6/7/8/9 + assertion-density floor + allowlist hygiene); zero-violation baseline; `--advisory` lists the review-only signals; `--json` for tooling. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/power-of-10-allowlist.json` | The single reasoned allowlist read by both the scanner and `PowerOfTenBoundaryTests`; every entry names the invariant that makes the exemption safe. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/doc-coverage-scan.py` | Scans Swift sources for type declarations with no adjacent `///` doc comment. Zero undocumented is the enforced baseline; runs in the Power-of-10 CI workflow. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/render-imessage-icon.py` | Renders the Messages extension's `iMessage App Icon` set (all twelve slots, exact pixel sizes, no alpha) from the Home Screen icon: lifts the sprig off its measured cream background, turns it onto the 4:3 diagonal, and scales it to the largest size that keeps 6% clearance inside the Messages "+" menu's superellipse mask (measured on the iOS 26.5 simulator). The set's source of truth — re-run it if the Home Screen icon changes; deterministic. The only script that needs Pillow + NumPy. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/sync-string-catalogs.sh` | Repopulates the `.xcstrings` catalogs outside Xcode via `xcstringstool sync` over the `.stringsdata` a build emits. Run after changing user-facing strings and commit the catalog diff with the code; `--check` for CI. Additive — vanished keys are marked stale, never deleted. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/run-gated-suites.sh` | Every CI test step's runner (P5 review finding 4): runs the named suites against the already-built bundle with `-resultBundlePath`, then reads the bundle's own `totalTestCount` and refuses a green run under the step's floor — because `-only-testing:` matches suite names exactly, matches nothing on a misspelling or a deleted suite, and then prints `TEST EXECUTE SUCCEEDED` over zero tests. `CIGateSelectorBoundaryTests` is the static half. Usable locally: `Scripts/run-gated-suites.sh <label> <min> <Suite>…`. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/run-gated-suites-selftest.sh` | Negative test proving the restart guard in `run-gated-suites.sh` is load-bearing on its LIVE branch, not only at its `--check-log` seam (P10 item 5): stubs `xcodebuild` and `xcrun` on `PATH` so the gate reads a green-looking run whose count clears the floor, and asserts the script still exits non-zero when the run output carries `Restarting after unexpected exit, crash, or test timeout` — a restart re-runs suites, so the bundle's `totalTestCount` is inflated and every floor above it stops proving anything. Four assertions: live negative/positive and seam negative/positive. Needs no Simulator, no build and no Xcode; runs on the enforcement self-test step of `s3-wall.yml`. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/release-checksum.sh` | Builds a release archive, emits SHA-256 checksums, and cuts a signed tag ([Release-Process.md](Release-Process.md), [Verifiability.md](Verifiability.md) §2). Honest limit, stated in the script itself: byte-exact verification of an App Store binary is impossible — Apple re-signs and re-encrypts every submission. What this gives is the self-build baseline: the signed tag pins the source, the checksums let two independent builders compare for drift, and a user can sideload their own build of the tag. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/spm-wall-selftest.sh` | Negative test proving the wall is load-bearing: plants a forbidden `import PrivateHealthStore` in `AIProviders`, asserts the build fails, then reverts. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/install-git-hooks.sh` | Once per clone: points `core.hooksPath` at the versioned `Scripts/git-hooks` so pushes run the wall check. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/git-hooks/pre-push` | Versioned pre-push hook: runs `power-of-10-scan.py` on every push and `spm-wall-check.sh` when a push touches any Swift/manifest/project file. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/branded-catalog/` | Python pipeline converting the USDA branded dataset into the curated and On-Demand-Resource SQLite catalogs. |
| `Fernlet/.github/workflows/s3-wall.yml` | CI workflow running the wall enforcement self-test, the seven wall suites and — since 2026-09-06 — the three mesh acceptance batteries (28 suites) on push/PR to `main`; every test step goes through `Scripts/run-gated-suites.sh`, so a step that ran zero tests cannot pass. **2026-09-21 (the cutover round, item 2a):** the "Resolve a simulator destination" step is a hard pin — `iPhone 17` or the job fails in seconds, no fallback to another device (plan §17.2.3 finding 6); read by `CIGateSelectorBoundaryTests.theSimulatorDestinationIsPinnedWithNoFallback`. **2026-09-24 (CI breadth):** five walls that ran on no workflow get lines of their own — `privacy-wipe` (`PrivacyWipeCoverageTests`, its two behaviour suites, `PersistedSurfaceWipeBoundaryTests`), `accessibility`, `memory-lifecycle`, `messages-extension`, `codeowners` — and thirteen names join the mesh line: the newest mesh security suites (seat transport proof, sid claim, returning-member re-seat), the resume copy and presentation tables, the wipe's identity effect, the host pin, and six wire goldens. Floors MEASURED on a clean build of the final integration head: privacy-wipe 42, accessibility 20, memory-lifecycle 8, messages-extension 5, codeowners 2, and mesh-batteries 1226 → 1353. **2026-09-24 (the owner-decisions round that followed):** `CompanionEmotionPrivacyTests` joins the `s3-grep` line (floor stays the shared 1), and two exchange-wire lines are added — `messages-envelope` (the version-2 Messages envelope, the multipart packet and the exchange core's version-1 goldens; floor MEASURED 46) and `recipe-wire` (`RecipeMultipartWireTests`, the multipart `fernlet.recipe` golden; floor MEASURED 19). **2026-09-29 (decimal ingredient grams):** `recipe-wire` 19 → 23, MEASURED — the four tests pinning the optional `preciseMacros` ingredient key (own row only, whole grams for an older reader, never in the exchange packet). **2026-09-30 (the Messages blank-screen round):** `MessagesReceivedItemTests` joins the `messages-envelope` line (floor MEASURED 46 → 53, 4 suites), and the `messages-extension` floor is RE-MEASURED 5 → 6 for `MessagesExtensionBoundaryTests`' new received-card pin. **2026-09-30 (ingredient-search round, fix round 1):** a `food-search` line runs the F10 measuring stick and the catalog corrections it measures — `IngredientSearchCorpusTests`, `CatalogDefaultUnitSweepTests`, `RecipeImportIngredientLineTests`, `BundledRowCorrectionTests`, `CatalogHygieneTests`, `DishTemplateBindAuditTests`; floor MEASURED 70 — since F5 also `FoodIngredientIdentityTests` (floor 98 by its fix round 2), and since F4b `TypicalPortionTableTests` and `RecipePortionPickerTests` (floor re-measured 112, then 119 at its fix round 1); since F9b the four correction-memory suites of `FoodSearchCorrectionMemoryTests.swift`, where recipe picks are pinned (floor re-measured 161, then 165 at its fix round 1). **2026-09-30 (the recipe-share round):** `MessagesRecipeCardParityTests` joins the `messages-envelope` line (floor MEASURED 53 → 57, 5 suites), and the `messages-extension` floor is RE-MEASURED 6 → 10 for `MessagesExtensionBoundaryTests`' shared-card-builder pins. **2026-10-02 (ProximityKit plan step A0.2):** the `crypto-goldens` line gains `ProximityNamespaceGoldenTests` and `ProximityNamespaceSoundnessTests` (floor MEASURED 38 → 170 across the step), and the `s3-grep` line gains `ProximityAuditBridgeTests` (A0.2.10) and `ProximityNamespaceBoundaryTests` (A0.2.12), its floor the shared 1. **2026-10-02 (ProximityKit plan step A0.3):** the `crypto-goldens` line gains `ProximityVocabularyGoldenTests` (floor MEASURED 170 → 199, 6 suites), then 199 → 211 (MEASURED) for the namespace vocabulary's cells: seven in `ProximityNamespaceSoundnessTests`, four in `ProximityVocabularyGoldenTests`, one in `ProximityNamespaceGoldenTests`, then 211 → 213 (MEASURED) for the two presentation-string consumer cells in `ProximityVocabularyGoldenTests`, then 213 → 216 (MEASURED) for its three record-kind and routed-type consumer cells, then 216 → 220 (MEASURED) for its four envelope, coordinator and mesh consumer cells; the `s3-grep` step's note counts the boundary wall's `fernlet` allowlist at 38 literals in 13 files. |
| `Fernlet/.github/workflows/power-of-10.yml` | CI workflow running the Power-of-10 mechanical scan, the doc-coverage scan and — since 2026-09-24 — the accessibility scanner (`Scripts/accessibility-scan.py`, the canonical half of the wall `AccessibilityBoundaryTests` ports, pinned by that suite's `theScannerRunsInCI`) on push/PR to `main` (Ubuntu, no Xcode). |
